# Frosthaven card halves

Every ability card of Frosthaven's 17 classes, both halves, as the game's own rule files define them (`Frosthaven_Data/StreamingAssets/Rulebase`, extracted to `extracted/Base`). Abilities read `Verb amount [modifiers] → sub-abilities`; `{...}` holds a verb's own settings.

17 classes, 504 cards, 1008 halves. Generated by `tools/mechanics.py`; do not edit by hand.
